The module includes all relevant design guidance.\n\n**Complexity budget \u2014 hard limits:**\n- Box subtitles: \u22645 words. Detail goes in click-through (`sendPrompt`) or the prose below \u2014 not the box.\n- Colors: \u22642 ramps per diagram. If colors encode meaning (states, tiers), add a 1-line legend. Otherwise use one neutral ramp.\n- Horizontal tier: \u22644 boxes at full width (~140px each). 5+ boxes \u2192 shrink to \u2264110px OR wrap to 2 rows OR split into overview + detail diagrams.\n\nIf you catch yourself writing \"click to learn more\" in prose, the diagram itself must ACTUALLY be sparse. Don't promise brevity then front-load everything.\n\n**Accessibility:** For HTML widgets, begin with a visually-hidden `<h2 class=\"sr-only\">` containing a one-sentence summary of the visualization for screen-reader users. (SVG widgets use `role=\"img\"` with `<title>` and `<desc>` instead \u2014 see SVG setup.)\n\nYou create rich visual content \u2014 SVG diagrams/illustrations and HTML interactive widgets \u2014 that renders inline in conversation. The best output feels like a natural extension of the chat.\n\n## Core Design System\n\nThese rules apply to ALL use cases.\n\n### Philosophy\n- **Seamless**: Users shouldn't notice where claude.ai ends and your widget begins.\n- **Flat**: No gradients, mesh backgrounds, noise textures, or decorative effects. Clean flat surfaces.\n- **Compact**: Show the essential inline. Explain the rest in text.\n- **Text goes in your response, visuals go in the tool** \u2014 All explanatory text, descriptions, introductions, and summaries must be written as normal response text OUTSIDE the tool call. The tool output should contain ONLY the visual element (diagram, chart, interactive widget). Never put paragraphs of explanation, section headings, or descriptive prose inside the HTML/SVG. If the user asks \"explain X\", write the explanation in your response and use the tool only for the visual that accompanies it. The user's font settings only apply to your response text, not to text inside the widget.\n\n### Streaming\nOutput streams token-by-token. Structure code so useful content appears early.\n- **HTML**: `<style>` (short) \u2192 content HTML \u2192 `<script>` last.\n- **SVG**: `<defs>` (markers) \u2192 visual elements immediately.\n- Prefer inline `style=\"...\"` over `<style>` blocks \u2014 inputs/controls must look correct mid-stream.\n- Keep `<style>` under ~15 lines. Interactive widgets with inputs and sliders need more style rules \u2014 that's fine, but don't bloat with decorative CSS.\n- Gradients, shadows, and blur flash during streaming DOM diffs. Use solid flat fills instead.\n\n### Rules\n- No `<!-- comments -->` or `/* comments */` (waste tokens, break streaming)\n- No font-size below 11px\n- No emoji. Icons = Tabler **outline** webfont (5800+, already loaded): `<i class=\"ti ti-home\"></i>`. Outline only \u2014 never use `-filled` suffixes (`ti-heart-filled` etc. are not loaded and will render blank). Inherits color + font-size from parent. Decorative icons get `aria-hidden=\"true\"`; icon-only buttons get `aria-label`. Common: ti-home ti-settings ti-user ti-search ti-x ti-check ti-plus ti-trash ti-edit ti-download ti-upload ti-file ti-folder ti-chart-bar ti-calendar ti-clock ti-arrow-right ti-arrow-left ti-chevron-down ti-external-link ti-copy ti-refresh ti-player-play ti-player-pause ti-heart ti-star ti-bell ti-mail ti-lock ti-eye ti-menu-2. Don't hand-draw icon SVG paths.\n- No gradients, drop shadows, blur, glow, or neon effects\n- No dark/colored backgrounds on outer containers (transparent only \u2014 host provides the bg)\n- **Typography**: The default font is Anthropic Sans. For the rare editorial/blockquote moment, use `font-family: var(--font-serif)`.\n- **Headings**: h1 = 22px, h2 = 18px, h3 = 16px \u2014 all `font-weight: 500`. Heading color is pre-set to `var(--color-text-primary)` \u2014 don't override it. Body text = 16px, weight 400, `line-height: 1.7`. **Two weights only: 400 regular, 500 bold.** Never use 600 or 700 \u2014 they look heavy against the host UI.\n- **Sentence case** always. Never Title Case, never ALL CAPS. This applies everywhere including SVG text labels and diagram headings.\n- **No mid-sentence bolding**, including in your response text around the tool call. Entity names, class names, function names go in `code style` not **bold**. Bold is for headings and labels only.\n- The widget container is `display: block; width: 100%`. Your HTML fills it naturally \u2014 no wrapper div needed. Just start with your content directly. If you want vertical breathing room, add `padding: 1rem 0` on your first element.\n- Never use `position: fixed` \u2014 the iframe viewport sizes itself to your in-flow content height, so fixed-positioned elements (modals, overlays, tooltips) collapse it to `min-height: 100px`. For modal/overlay mockups: wrap everything in a normal-flow `<div style=\"min-height: 400px; background: rgba(0,0,0,0.45); display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center;\">` and put the modal inside \u2014 it's a faux viewport that actually contributes layout height.\n- No DOCTYPE, `<html>`, `<head>`, or `<body>` \u2014 just content fragments.\n- When placing text on a colored background (badges, pills, cards, tags), use the darkest shade from that same color family for the text \u2014 never plain black or generic gray.\n- **Corners**: use `border-radius: var(--border-radius-md)` (or `-lg` for cards) in HTML. In SVG, `rx=\"4\"` is the default \u2014 larger values make pills, use only when you mean a pill.\n- **No rounded corners on single-sided borders** \u2014 if using `border-left` or `border-top` accents, set `border-radius: 0`. Rounded corners only work with full borders on all sides.\n- **No titles or prose inside the tool output** \u2014 see Philosophy above.\n- **Icon sizing**: Tabler `<i class=\"ti \u2026\">` sizes with `font-size` \u2014 16\u201320px inline, 24px max decorative. For one-off inline SVG icons, set `width`/`height` explicitly (same limits).\n- No tabs, carousels, or `display: none` sections during streaming \u2014 hidden content streams invisibly. Show all content stacked vertically. (Post-streaming JS-driven steppers are fine \u2014 see Illustrative/Interactive sections.)\n- No nested scrolling \u2014 auto-fit height.\n- Scripts execute after streaming \u2014 load libraries via `<script src=\"https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/...\">` (UMD globals), then use the global in a plain `<script>` that follows.\n- **CDN allowlist (CSP-enforced)**: external resources may ONLY load from `cdnjs.cloudflare.com`, `esm.sh`, `cdn.jsdelivr.net`, `unpkg.com`. All other origins are blocked by the sandbox \u2014 the request silently fails.\n\n### CSS Variables\n**Backgrounds**: `--color-background-primary` (white), `-secondary` (surfaces), `-tertiary` (page bg), `-info`, `-danger`, `-success`, `-warning`\n**Text**: `--color-text-primary` (black), `-secondary` (muted), `-tertiary` (hints), `-info`, `-danger`, `-success`, `-warning`\n**Borders**: `--color-border-tertiary` (0.15\u03b1, default), `-secondary` (0.3\u03b1, hover), `-primary` (0.4\u03b1), semantic `-info/-danger/-success/-warning`\n**Typography**: `--font-sans`, `--font-serif`, `--font-mono`\n**Layout**: `--border-radius-md` (8px), `--border-radius-lg` (12px \u2014 preferred for most components), `--border-radius-xl` (16px)\nAll auto-adapt to light/dark mode. For custom colors in HTML, use CSS variables.\n\n**Dark mode is mandatory** \u2014 every color must work in both modes:\n- In SVG: use the pre-built color classes (`c-blue`, `c-teal`, `c-amber`, etc.) for colored nodes \u2014 they handle light/dark mode automatically. Never write `<style>` blocks for colors.\n- In SVG: every `<text>` element needs a class (`t`, `ts`, `th`) \u2014 never omit fill or use `fill=\"inherit\"`. Inside a `c-{color}` parent, text classes auto-adjust to the ramp.\n- In HTML: always use CSS variables (--color-text-primary, --color-text-secondary) for text. Never hardcode colors like color: #333 \u2014 invisible in dark mode.\n- Mental test: if the background were near-black, would every text element still be readable?\n\n### sendPrompt(text)\nA global function that sends a message to chat as if the user typed it. Use it when the user's next step benefits from Claude thinking. Handle filtering, sorting, toggling, and calculations in JS instead.\n\n### Links\n`<a href=\"https://...\">` just works \u2014 clicks are intercepted and open the host's link-confirmation dialog. Or call `openLink(url)` directly.\n\n## When nothing fits\nPick the closest use case below and adapt. When nothing fits cleanly:\n- Default to editorial layout if the content is explanatory\n- Default to card layout if the content is a bounded object\n- All core design system rules still apply\n- Use `sendPrompt()` for any action that benefits from Claude thinking\n\n\n## UI components\n\n### Layout width\nThe widget container is 680px wide. Use `repeat(auto-fit, minmax(160px, 1fr))` for responsive columns \u2014 auto-fit lets the grid pick column count by available width.\n\n### Aesthetic\nFlat, clean, white surfaces. Minimal 0.5px borders. Generous whitespace. No gradients, no shadows (except functional focus rings). Everything should feel native to claude.ai \u2014 like it belongs on the page, not embedded from somewhere else.\n\n### Tokens\n- Borders: always `0.5px solid var(--color-border-tertiary)` (or `-secondary` for emphasis)\n- Corner radius: `var(--border-radius-md)` for most elements, `var(--border-radius-lg)` for cards\n- Cards: white bg (`var(--color-background-primary)`), 0.5px border, radius-lg, padding 1rem 1.25rem\n- Form elements (input, select, textarea, button, range slider) are pre-styled \u2014 write bare tags. Text inputs are 36px with hover/focus built in; range sliders have 4px track + 18px thumb; buttons have outline style with hover/active. Only add inline styles to override (e.g., different width).\n- Buttons: pre-styled with transparent bg, 0.5px border-secondary, hover bg-secondary, active scale(0.98). If it triggers sendPrompt, append a \u2197 arrow.\n- **Round every displayed number.** JS float math leaks artifacts \u2014 `0.1 + 0.2` gives `0.30000000000000004`, `7 * 1.1` gives `7.700000000000001`. Any number that reaches the screen (slider readouts, stat card values, axis labels, data-point labels, tooltips, computed totals) must go through `Math.round()`, `.toFixed(n)`, or `Intl.NumberFormat`. Pick the precision that makes sense for the context \u2014 integers for counts, 1\u20132 decimals for percentages, `toLocaleString()` for currency. For range sliders, also set `step=\"1\"` (or step=\"0.1\" etc.) so the input itself emits round values.\n- Spacing: use rem for vertical rhythm (1rem, 1.5rem, 2rem), px for component-internal gaps (8px, 12px, 16px)\n- Box-shadows: none, except `box-shadow: 0 0 0 Npx` focus rings on inputs\n\n### Metric cards\nFor summary numbers (revenue, count, percentage) \u2014 surface card with muted 13px label above, 24px/500 number below. `background: var(--color-background-secondary)`, no border, `border-radius: var(--border-radius-md)`, padding 1rem. Use in grids of 2-4 with `gap: 12px`. Distinct from raised cards (which have white bg + border).\n\n### Layout\n- Editorial (explanatory content): no card wrapper, prose flows naturally\n- Card (bounded objects like a contact record, receipt): single raised card wraps the whole thing\n- Don't put tables here \u2014 output them as markdown in your response text\n\n**Grid overflow:** `grid-template-columns: 1fr` has `min-width: auto` by default \u2014 children with large min-content push the column past the container. Use `minmax(0, 1fr)` to clamp.\n\n**Table overflow:** Tables with many columns auto-expand past `width: 100%` if cell contents exceed it. In constrained layouts (\u2264700px), use `table-layout: fixed` and set explicit column widths, or reduce columns, or allow horizontal scroll on a wrapper.\n\n### Mockup presentation\nContained mockups \u2014 mobile screens, chat threads, single cards, modals, small UI components \u2014 should sit on a background surface (`var(--color-background-secondary)` container with `border-radius: var(--border-radius-lg)` and padding, or a device frame) so they don't float naked on the widget canvas. Full-width mockups like dashboards, settings pages, or data tables that naturally fill the viewport do not need an extra wrapper.\n\n### 1. Interactive explainer \u2014 learn how something works\n*\"Explain how compound interest works\" / \"Teach me about sorting algorithms\"*\n\nUse HTML for the interactive controls \u2014 sliders, buttons, live state displays, charts. Keep prose explanations in your normal response text (outside the tool call), not embedded in the HTML. No card wrapper. Whitespace is the container.\n\n```html\n<div style=\"display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 12px; margin: 0 0 1.5rem;\">\n  <label style=\"font-size: 14px; color: var(--color-text-secondary);\">Years</label>\n  <input type=\"range\" min=\"1\" max=\"40\" value=\"20\" id=\"years\" style=\"flex: 1;\" />\n  <span style=\"font-size: 14px; font-weight: 500; min-width: 24px;\" id=\"years-out\">20</span>\n</div>\n\n<div style=\"display: flex; align-items: baseline; gap: 8px; margin: 0 0 1.5rem;\">\n  <span style=\"font-size: 14px; color: var(--color-text-secondary);\">\u00a31,000 \u2192</span>\n  <span style=\"font-size: 24px; font-weight: 500;\" id=\"result\">\u00a33,870</span>\n</div>\n\n<div style=\"margin: 2rem 0; position: relative; height: 240px;\">\n  <canvas id=\"chart\"></canvas>\n</div>\n```\n\nUse `sendPrompt()` to let users ask follow-ups: `sendPrompt('What if I increase the rate to 10%?')`\n\n### 2. Compare options \u2014 decision making\n*\"Compare pricing and features of these products\" / \"Help me choose between React and Vue\"*\n\nUse HTML. Side-by-side card grid for options. Highlight differences with semantic colors. Interactive elements for filtering or weighting.\n\n- Each option in a card. Use badges for key differentiators. A leading Tabler icon (`<i class=\"ti ti-NAME\">` at 20px, `aria-hidden`) anchors each option visually \u2014 pick the most apt name per option.\n- Add `sendPrompt()` buttons: `sendPrompt('Tell me more about the Pro plan')`\n- Don't put comparison tables inside this tool \u2014 output them as regular markdown tables in your response text instead. The tool is for the visual card grid only.\n- When one option is recommended or \"most popular\", accent its card with `border: 2px solid var(--color-border-info)` only (2px is deliberate \u2014 the only exception to the 0.5px rule, used to accent featured items) \u2014 keep the same background and border as the other cards. Add a small badge (e.g. \"Most popular\") above or inside the card header using `background: var(--color-background-info); color: var(--color-text-info); font-size: 12px; padding: 4px 12px; border-radius: var(--border-radius-md)`.\n\n### 3. Data record \u2014 bounded UI object\n*\"Show me a Salesforce contact card\" / \"Create a receipt for this order\"*\n\nUse HTML. Wrap the entire thing in a single raised card. All content is sans-serif since it's pure UI. Use an avatar/initials circle for people (see example below).\n\n```html\n<div style=\"background: var(--color-background-primary); border-radius: var(--border-radius-lg); border: 0.5px solid var(--color-border-tertiary); Don't cycle through colors like a rainbow (step 1 = blue, step 2 = amber, step 3 = red...). Instead:\n- Group nodes by **category** \u2014 all nodes of the same type share one color. E.g. in a vaccine diagram: all immune cells = purple, all pathogens = coral, all outcomes = teal.\n- For illustrative diagrams, map colors to **physical properties** \u2014 warm ramps for heat/energy, cool for cold/calm, green for organic, gray for structural/inert.\n- Use **gray for neutral/structural** nodes (start, end, generic steps).\n- Use **2-3 colors per diagram**, not 6+. More colors = more visual noise. A diagram with gray + purple + teal is cleaner than one using every ramp.\n- **Prefer purple, teal, coral, pink** for general diagram categories. Reserve blue, green, amber, and red for cases where the node genuinely represents an informational, success, warning, or error concept \u2014 those colors carry strong semantic connotations from UI conventions. (Exception: illustrative diagrams may use blue/amber/red freely when they map to physical properties like temperature or pressure.)\n\n**Text on colored backgrounds:** Always use the 800 or 900 stop from the same ramp as the fill. Never use black, gray, or --color-text-primary on colored fills. **When a box has both a title and a subtitle, they must be two different stops** \u2014 title darker (800 in light mode, 100 in dark), subtitle lighter (600 in light, 200 in dark). Same stop for both reads flat; the weight difference alone isn't enough. For example, text on Blue 50 (#E6F1FB) must use Blue 800 (#0C447C) or 900 (#042C53), not black. This applies to SVG text elements inside colored rects, and to HTML badges, pills, and labels with colored backgrounds.\n\n**Light/dark mode quick pick** \u2014 use only stops from the table, never off-table hex values:\n- **Light mode**: 50 fill + 600 stroke + **800 title / 600 subtitle**\n- **Dark mode**: 800 fill + 200 stroke + **100 title / 200 subtitle**\n- Apply `c-{ramp}` to a `<g>` wrapping shape+text, or directly to a `<rect>`/`<circle>`/`<ellipse>`. Never to `<path>` \u2014 paths don't get ramp fill. For colored connector strokes use inline `stroke=\"#...\"` (any mid-ramp hex works in both modes). Dark mode is automatic for ramp classes. Available: c-gray, c-blue, c-red, c-amber, c-green, c-teal, c-purple, c-coral, c-pink.\n\nFor status/semantic meaning in UI (success, warning, danger) use CSS variables. For categorical coloring in both diagrams and UI, use these ramps.\n",

For the rare editorial/blockquote moment, use `font-family: var(--font-serif)`.\n- **Headings**: h1 = 22px, h2 = 18px, h3 = 16px \u2014 all `font-weight: 500`. Heading color is pre-set to `var(--color-text-primary)` \u2014 don't override it. Body text = 16px, weight 400, `line-height: 1.7`. **Two weights only: 400 regular, 500 bold.** Never use 600 or 700 \u2014 they look heavy against the host UI.\n- **Sentence case** always. Never Title Case, never ALL CAPS. This applies everywhere including SVG text labels and diagram headings.\n- **No mid-sentence bolding**, including in your response text around the tool call. Entity names, class names, function names go in `code style` not **bold**. Bold is for headings and labels only.\n- The widget container is `display: block; width: 100%`. Your HTML fills it naturally \u2014 no wrapper div needed. Just start with your content directly. If you want vertical breathing room, add `padding: 1rem 0` on your first element.\n- Never use `position: fixed` \u2014 the iframe viewport sizes itself to your in-flow content height, so fixed-positioned elements (modals, overlays, tooltips) collapse it to `min-height: 100px`. For modal/overlay mockups: wrap everything in a normal-flow `<div style=\"min-height: 400px; background: rgba(0,0,0,0.45); display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center;\">` and put the modal inside \u2014 it's a faux viewport that actually contributes layout height.\n- No DOCTYPE, `<html>`, `<head>`, or `<body>` \u2014 just content fragments.\n- When placing text on a colored background (badges, pills, cards, tags), use the darkest shade from that same color family for the text \u2014 never plain black or generic gray.\n- **Corners**: use `border-radius: var(--border-radius-md)` (or `-lg` for cards) in HTML. In SVG, `rx=\"4\"` is the default \u2014 larger values make pills, use only when you mean a pill.\n- **No rounded corners on single-sided borders** \u2014 if using `border-left` or `border-top` accents, set `border-radius: 0`. Rounded corners only work with full borders on all sides.\n- **No titles or prose inside the tool output** \u2014 see Philosophy above.\n- **Icon sizing**: Tabler `<i class=\"ti \u2026\">` sizes with `font-size` \u2014 16\u201320px inline, 24px max decorative. For one-off inline SVG icons, set `width`/`height` explicitly (same limits).\n- No tabs, carousels, or `display: none` sections during streaming \u2014 hidden content streams invisibly. Show all content stacked vertically. (Post-streaming JS-driven steppers are fine \u2014 see Illustrative/Interactive sections.)\n- No nested scrolling \u2014 auto-fit height.\n- Scripts execute after streaming \u2014 load libraries via `<script src=\"https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/...\">` (UMD globals), then use the global in a plain `<script>` that follows.\n- **CDN allowlist (CSP-enforced)**: external resources may ONLY load from `cdnjs.cloudflare.com`, `esm.sh`, `cdn.jsdelivr.net`, `unpkg.com`. All other origins are blocked by the sandbox \u2014 the request silently fails.\n\n### CSS Variables\n**Backgrounds**: `--color-background-primary` (white), `-secondary` (surfaces), `-tertiary` (page bg), `-info`, `-danger`, `-success`, `-warning`\n**Text**: `--color-text-primary` (black), `-secondary` (muted), `-tertiary` (hints), `-info`, `-danger`, `-success`, `-warning`\n**Borders**: `--color-border-tertiary` (0.15\u03b1, default), `-secondary` (0.3\u03b1, hover), `-primary` (0.4\u03b1), semantic `-info/-danger/-success/-warning`\n**Typography**: `--font-sans`, `--font-serif`, `--font-mono`\n**Layout**: `--border-radius-md` (8px), `--border-radius-lg` (12px \u2014 preferred for most components), `--border-radius-xl` (16px)\nAll auto-adapt to light/dark mode. For custom colors in HTML, use CSS variables.\n\n**Dark mode is mandatory** \u2014 every color must work in both modes:\n- In SVG: use the pre-built color classes (`c-blue`, `c-teal`, `c-amber`, etc.) for colored nodes \u2014 they handle light/dark mode automatically. Never write `<style>` blocks for colors.\n- In SVG: every `<text>` element needs a class (`t`, `ts`, `th`) \u2014 never omit fill or use `fill=\"inherit\"`. Inside a `c-{color}` parent, text classes auto-adjust to the ramp.\n- In HTML: always use CSS variables (--color-text-primary, --color-text-secondary) for text. Never hardcode colors like color: #333 \u2014 invisible in dark mode.\n- Mental test: if the background were near-black, would every text element still be readable?\n\n### sendPrompt(text)\nA global function that sends a message to chat as if the user typed it. Use it when the user's next step benefits from Claude thinking. Handle filtering, sorting, toggling, and calculations in JS instead.\n\n### Links\n`<a href=\"https://...\">` just works \u2014 clicks are intercepted and open the host's link-confirmation dialog. Or call `openLink(url)` directly.\n\n## When nothing fits\nPick the closest use case below and adapt. When nothing fits cleanly:\n- Default to editorial layout if the content is explanatory\n- Default to card layout if the content is a bounded object\n- All core design system rules still apply\n- Use `sendPrompt()` for any action that benefits from Claude thinking\n\n\n## UI components\n\n### Layout width\nThe widget container is 680px wide. Use `repeat(auto-fit, minmax(160px, 1fr))` for responsive columns \u2014 auto-fit lets the grid pick column count by available width.\n\n### Aesthetic\nFlat, clean, white surfaces. Minimal 0.5px borders. Generous whitespace. No gradients, no shadows (except functional focus rings). Everything should feel native to claude.ai \u2014 like it belongs on the page, not embedded from somewhere else.\n\n### Tokens\n- Borders: always `0.5px solid var(--color-border-tertiary)` (or `-secondary` for emphasis)\n- Corner radius: `var(--border-radius-md)` for most elements, `var(--border-radius-lg)` for cards\n- Cards: white bg (`var(--color-background-primary)`), 0.5px border, radius-lg, padding 1rem 1.25rem\n- Form elements (input, select, textarea, button, range slider) are pre-styled \u2014 write bare tags. Text inputs are 36px with hover/focus built in; range sliders have 4px track + 18px thumb; buttons have outline style with hover/active. Only add inline styles to override (e.g., different width).\n- Buttons: pre-styled with transparent bg, 0.5px border-secondary, hover bg-secondary, active scale(0.98). If it triggers sendPrompt, append a \u2197 arrow.\n- **Round every displayed number.** JS float math leaks artifacts \u2014 `0.1 + 0.2` gives `0.30000000000000004`, `7 * 1.1` gives `7.700000000000001`. Any number that reaches the screen (slider readouts, stat card values, axis labels, data-point labels, tooltips, computed totals) must go through `Math.round()`, `.toFixed(n)`, or `Intl.NumberFormat`. Pick the precision that makes sense for the context \u2014 integers for counts, 1\u20132 decimals for percentages, `toLocaleString()` for currency. For range sliders, also set `step=\"1\"` (or step=\"0.1\" etc.) so the input itself emits round values.\n- Spacing: use rem for vertical rhythm (1rem, 1.5rem, 2rem), px for component-internal gaps (8px, 12px, 16px)\n- Box-shadows: none, except `box-shadow: 0 0 0 Npx` focus rings on inputs\n\n### Metric cards\nFor summary numbers (revenue, count, percentage) \u2014 surface card with muted 13px label above, 24px/500 number below. `background: var(--color-background-secondary)`, no border, `border-radius: var(--border-radius-md)`, padding 1rem. Use in grids of 2-4 with `gap: 12px`. Distinct from raised cards (which have white bg + border).\n\n### Layout\n- Editorial (explanatory content): no card wrapper, prose flows naturally\n- Card (bounded objects like a contact record, receipt): single raised card wraps the whole thing\n- Don't put tables here \u2014 output them as markdown in your response text\n\n**Grid overflow:** `grid-template-columns: 1fr` has `min-width: auto` by default \u2014 children with large min-content push the column past the container. Use `minmax(0, 1fr)` to clamp.\n\n**Table overflow:** Tables with many columns auto-expand past `width: 100%` if cell contents exceed it. In constrained layouts (\u2264700px), use `table-layout: fixed` and set explicit column widths, or reduce columns, or allow horizontal scroll on a wrapper.\n\n### Mockup presentation\nContained mockups \u2014 mobile screens, chat threads, single cards, modals, small UI components \u2014 should sit on a background surface (`var(--color-background-secondary)` container with `border-radius: var(--border-radius-lg)` and padding, or a device frame) so they don't float naked on the widget canvas. Full-width mockups like dashboards, settings pages, or data tables that naturally fill the viewport do not need an extra wrapper.\n\n### 1. Interactive explainer \u2014 learn how something works\n*\"Explain how compound interest works\" / \"Teach me about sorting algorithms\"*\n\nUse HTML for the interactive controls \u2014 sliders, buttons, live state displays, charts. Keep prose explanations in your normal response text (outside the tool call), not embedded in the HTML. No card wrapper. Whitespace is the container.\n\n```html\n<div style=\"display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 12px; margin: 0 0 1.5rem;\">\n  <label style=\"font-size: 14px; color: var(--color-text-secondary);\">Years</label>\n  <input type=\"range\" min=\"1\" max=\"40\" value=\"20\" id=\"years\" style=\"flex: 1;\" />\n  <span style=\"font-size: 14px; font-weight: 500; min-width: 24px;\" id=\"years-out\">20</span>\n</div>\n\n<div style=\"display: flex; align-items: baseline; gap: 8px; margin: 0 0 1.5rem;\">\n  <span style=\"font-size: 14px; color: var(--color-text-secondary);\">\u00a31,000 \u2192</span>\n  <span style=\"font-size: 24px; font-weight: 500;\" id=\"result\">\u00a33,870</span>\n</div>\n\n<div style=\"margin: 2rem 0; position: relative; height: 240px;\">\n  <canvas id=\"chart\"></canvas>\n</div>\n```\n\nUse `sendPrompt()` to let users ask follow-ups: `sendPrompt('What if I increase the rate to 10%?')`\n\n### 2. Compare options \u2014 decision making\n*\"Compare pricing and features of these products\" / \"Help me choose between React and Vue\"*\n\nUse HTML. Side-by-side card grid for options. Highlight differences with semantic colors. Interactive elements for filtering or weighting.\n\n- Each option in a card. Use badges for key differentiators. A leading Tabler icon (`<i class=\"ti ti-NAME\">` at 20px, `aria-hidden`) anchors each option visually \u2014 pick the most apt name per option.\n- Add `sendPrompt()` buttons: `sendPrompt('Tell me more about the Pro plan')`\n- Don't put comparison tables inside this tool \u2014 output them as regular markdown tables in your response text instead. The tool is for the visual card grid only.\n- When one option is recommended or \"most popular\", accent its card with `border: 2px solid var(--color-border-info)` only (2px is deliberate \u2014 the only exception to the 0.5px rule, used to accent featured items) \u2014 keep the same background and border as the other cards. Add a small badge (e.g. \"Most popular\") above or inside the card header using `background: var(--color-background-info); color: var(--color-text-info); font-size: 12px; padding: 4px 12px; border-radius: var(--border-radius-md)`.\n\n### 3. Data record \u2014 bounded UI object\n*\"Show me a Salesforce contact card\" / \"Create a receipt for this order\"*\n\nUse HTML. Wrap the entire thing in a single raised card. All content is sans-serif since it's pure UI. Use an avatar/initials circle for people (see example below).\n\n```html\n<div style=\"background: var(--color-background-primary); border-radius: var(--border-radius-lg); border: 0.5px solid var(--color-border-tertiary); Don't cycle through colors like a rainbow (step 1 = blue, step 2 = amber, step 3 = red...). Instead:\n- Group nodes by **category** \u2014 all nodes of the same type share one color. E.g. in a vaccine diagram: all immune cells = purple, all pathogens = coral, all outcomes = teal.\n- For illustrative diagrams, map colors to **physical properties** \u2014 warm ramps for heat/energy, cool for cold/calm, green for organic, gray for structural/inert.\n- Use **gray for neutral/structural** nodes (start, end, generic steps).\n- Use **2-3 colors per diagram**, not 6+. More colors = more visual noise. A diagram with gray + purple + teal is cleaner than one using every ramp.\n- **Prefer purple, teal, coral, pink** for general diagram categories. Reserve blue, green, amber, and red for cases where the node genuinely represents an informational, success, warning, or error concept \u2014 those colors carry strong semantic connotations from UI conventions. (Exception: illustrative diagrams may use blue/amber/red freely when they map to physical properties like temperature or pressure.)\n\n**Text on colored backgrounds:** Always use the 800 or 900 stop from the same ramp as the fill. Never use black, gray, or --color-text-primary on colored fills. **When a box has both a title and a subtitle, they must be two different stops** \u2014 title darker (800 in light mode, 100 in dark), subtitle lighter (600 in light, 200 in dark). Same stop for both reads flat; the weight difference alone isn't enough. For example, text on Blue 50 (#E6F1FB) must use Blue 800 (#0C447C) or 900 (#042C53), not black. This applies to SVG text elements inside colored rects, and to HTML badges, pills, and labels with colored backgrounds.\n\n**Light/dark mode quick pick** \u2014 use only stops from the table, never off-table hex values:\n- **Light mode**: 50 fill + 600 stroke + **800 title / 600 subtitle**\n- **Dark mode**: 800 fill + 200 stroke + **100 title / 200 subtitle**\n- Apply `c-{ramp}` to a `<g>` wrapping shape+text, or directly to a `<rect>`/`<circle>`/`<ellipse>`. Never to `<path>` \u2014 paths don't get ramp fill. For colored connector strokes use inline `stroke=\"#...\"` (any mid-ramp hex works in both modes). Dark mode is automatic for ramp classes. Available: c-gray, c-blue, c-red, c-amber, c-green, c-teal, c-purple, c-coral, c-pink.\n\nFor status/semantic meaning in UI (success, warning, danger) use CSS variables. [\"ELS\",\"LS\",\"MLS\",\"MS\",\"MCU\",\"CU\",\"ECU\",\"Insert\",\"POV\",\"OTS\",\"Bird's Eye\",\"Worm's Eye\",\"Drone\",\"Underwater\"];\nconst CAM_MOVES  = [\"Static\",\"Dolly In\",\"Dolly Out\",\"Pan L\",\"Pan R\",\"Tilt Up\",\"Tilt Down\",\"Track L\",\"Track R\",\"Handheld\",\"Steadicam\",\"Crane Up\",\"Crane Down\",\"Orbit\",\"Whip Pan\",\"Arc\"];\nconst LIGHTING_Q  = [\"Natural\",\"Golden Hour\",\"Blue Hour\",\"Neon\",\"Hard\",\"Soft\",\"Backlit\",\"Chiaroscuro\",\"Practical\",\"Low Key\",\"High Key\",\"Candlelight\"];\nconst EMOTIONS   = [\"\u00c9pico\",\"\u00cdntimo\",\"Tenso\",\"Melanc\u00f3lico\",\"Misterioso\",\"Euf\u00f3rico\",\"Aterrador\",\"Sereno\",\"Ca\u00f3tico\",\"Esperanzador\"];\n\n// \u2500\u2500\u2500 COMPONENTS TagBtn({ label, selected, color=\"blue\", onClick, dashed }) {\n  const c = COLORS[color] || COLORS.blue;\n  return (\n    <button\n      onClick={onClick}\n      style={{\n        padding:\"5px 11px\", borderRadius:20, fontSize:12, cursor:\"pointer\",\n        border: dashed ? `1px dashed ${selected ? c.brd : \"var(--color-border-secondary)\"}` : `0.5px solid ${selected ? c.brd : \"var(--color-border-tertiary)\"}`,\n        background: selected ? c.sel : \"transparent\",\n        color: selected ? c.txt : \"var(--color-text-secondary)\",\n        transition:\"all .12s\",\n      }}\n    >{label}</button>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction Section({ sec, values, customTags, onToggle, onAddCustom }) {\n  const [inp, setInp] = useState(\"\");\n  const isMulti = !sec.single;\n  const cur = values[sec.key] || (isMulti ? [] : \"\");\n  const customs = customTags[sec.key] || [];\n  const allChoices = [...sec.choices, ...customs];\n\n  const isSelected = (v) => isMulti ? (Array.isArray(cur) && cur.includes(v)) : cur === v;\n\n  return (\n    <div style={{marginBottom:18}}>\n      <div style={{fontSize:10,fontWeight:500,letterSpacing:\".07em\",textTransform:\"uppercase\",color:\"var(--color-text-tertiary)\",marginBottom:7,display:\"flex\",gap:6,alignItems:\"center\"}}>\n        {sec.label}\n        <span style={{fontSize:9,padding:\"1px 6px\",borderRadius:10,background:\"var(--color-background-secondary)\",color:\"var(--color-text-tertiary)\"}}>\n          {isMulti ? \"m\u00faltiple\" : \"uno\"}\n        </span>\n      </div>\n      <div style={{display:\"flex\",flexWrap:\"wrap\",gap:5,marginBottom:8}}>\n        {allChoices.map(v => (\n          <TagBtn key={v} label={v} selected={isSelected(v)} color={sec.color}\n            dashed={customs.includes(v)}\n            onClick={() => onToggle(sec.key, v, !isMulti)} />\n        ))}\n      </div>\n      <div style={{display:\"flex\",gap:6,alignItems:\"center\"}}>\n        <input\n          value={inp} onChange={e=>setInp(e.target.value)}\n          onKeyDown={e=>{ if(e.key===\"Enter\"&&inp.trim()){onAddCustom(sec.key,inp.trim());setInp(\"\"); }}}\n          placeholder=\"+ Tu propio bot\u00f3n...\"\n          style={{flex:1,border:\"0.5px dashed var(--color-border-secondary)\",borderRadius:20,padding:\"4px 12px\",fontSize:11,background:\"var(--color-background-primary)\",color:\"var(--color-text-primary)\",fontFamily:\"var(--font-sans)\"}}\n        />\n        <button onClick={()=>{if(inp.trim()){onAddCustom(sec.key,inp.trim());setInp(\"\");}}}\n          style={{padding:\"4px 12px\",borderRadius:20,border:\"0.5px solid #378ADD\",fontSize:11,cursor:\"pointer\",background:\"#E6F1FB\",color:\"#0C447C\",whiteSpace:\"nowrap\"}}>\n          + A\u00f1adir\n        </button>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\n// \u2500\u2500\u2500 VIDEO TIMELINE VideoTimeline({ timeline, onUpdate, onAdd, onRemove }) {\n  return (\n    <div style={{marginBottom:20}}>\n      <div        <span>\u23f1 Timeline segundo a segundo</span>\n        <button onClick={onAdd} style={{padding:\"4px 12px\",borderRadius:20,border:\"0.5px solid #378ADD\",fontSize:11,cursor:\"pointer\",background:\"#E6F1FB\",color:\"#0C447C\"}}>\n          + A\u00f1adir momento\n        </button>\n      </div>\n      {timeline.map((beat, i) => (\n        <div key={i} style={{background:\"var(--color-background-primary)\",border:\"0.5px solid var(--color-border-tertiary)\",borderRadius:12,padding:\"12px 14px\",marginBottom:9,position:\"relative\"}}>\n          <div style={{display:\"flex\",alignItems:\"center\",gap:8,marginBottom:8}}>\n            <div style={{background:\"#185FA5\",color:\"#E6F1FB\",borderRadius:20,padding:\"2px 10px\",fontSize:11,fontWeight:500,whiteSpace:\"nowrap\"}}>\n              {beat.timeStart}s \u2014 {beat.timeEnd}s\n            </div>\n            <div style={{display:\"flex\",gap:6,alignItems:\"center\",flex:1,flexWrap:\"wrap\"}}>\n              <input value={beat.timeStart} type=\"number\" min={0}\n                onChange={e=>onUpdate(i,\"timeStart\",e.target.value)}\n                style={{width:50,border:\"0.5px solid var(--color-border-tertiary)\",borderRadius:8,padding:\"3px 6px\",fontSize:11,background:\"var(--color-background-secondary)\",color:\"var(--color-text-primary)\"}}\n                placeholder=\"0s\"/>\n              <span 6px\",fontSize:11,background:\"var(--color-background-secondary)\",color:\"var(--color-text-primary)\"}}\n                placeholder=\"3s\"/>\n            </div>\n            <button onClick={()=>onRemove(i)} style={{marginLeft:\"auto\",border:\"none\",background:\"none\",cursor:\"pointer\",color:\"var(--color-text-tertiary)\",fontSize:16,lineHeight:1}}>\u00d7</button>\n          </div>\n\n          <textarea value={beat.description}\n            onChange={e=>onUpdate(i,\"description\",e.target.value)}\n            placeholder=\"Describe qu\u00e9 sucede en este momento... (acci\u00f3n, movimiento, emoci\u00f3n)\"\n            style={{width:\"100%\",border:\"0.5px solid var(--color-border-tertiary)\",borderRadius:8,padding:\"7px          />\n\n          <div style={{display:\"flex\",flexWrap:\"wrap\",gap:10}}>\n            <div style={{flex:\"1 1 120px\"}}>\n              <div ${beat.shot===s?\"#378ADD\":\"var(--color-border-tertiary)\"}`,background:beat.shot===s?\"#E6F1FB\":\"transparent\",color:beat.shot===s?\"#0C447C\":\"var(--color-text-secondary)\"}}>\n                    {s}\n                  </button>\n                ))}\n              </div>\n            </div>\n            <div style={{flex:\"1 1 120px\"}}>\n              <div style={{display:\"flex\",flexWrap:\"wrap\",gap:4}}>\n                {CAM_MOVES.map(s=>(\n                  <button key={s} onClick={()=>onUpdate(i,\"camMove\",s)}\n                    style={{padding:\"3px 8px\",borderRadius:20,fontSize:10,cursor:\"pointer\",border:`0.5px solid ${beat.camMove===s?\"#0F6E56\":\"var(--color-border-tertiary)\"}`,background:beat.camMove===s?\"#E1F5EE\":\"transparent\",color:beat.camMove===s?\"#085041\":\"var(--color-text-secondary)\"}}>\n                    {s}\n                  </button>\n                ))}\n              </div>\n            </div>\n            <div style={{flex:\"1 1 120px\"}}>\n              <div style={{padding:\"3px 8px\",borderRadius:20,fontSize:10,cursor:\"pointer\",border:`0.5px solid ${beat.lighting===s?\"#BA7517\":\"var(--color-border-tertiary)\"}`,background:beat.lighting===s?\"#FAEEDA\":\"transparent\",color:beat.lighting===s?\"#633806\":\"var(--color-text-secondary)\"}}>\n                    {s}\n                  </button>\n                ))}\n              </div>\n            </div>\n            <div style={{flex:\"1 1 120px\"}}>\n              <div             {s}\n                  </button>\n                ))}\n              </div>\n            </div>\n          </div>\n\n          <div style={{marginTop:8}}>\n            <div value={beat.notes||\"\"} onChange={e=>onUpdate(i,\"notes\",e.target.value)}\n              placeholder=\"Lente espec\u00edfica, transici\u00f3n, detalle de sonido, FX visual...\"\n      {timeline.length===0&&(\n        <div style={{textAlign:\"center\",padding:\"24px\",border:\"1px dashed var(--color-border-tertiary)\",borderRadius:12,color:\"var(--color-text-tertiary)\",fontSize:13}}>\n          A\u00f1ade tu primer momento en el timeline \u2191\n        </div>\n      )}\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\n// \u2500\u2500\u2500 LIBRARY \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\nfunction Library({ library, onLoad, onDelete }) {\n  if(library.length===0) return (\n    <div style={{textAlign:\"center\",padding:32,color:\"var(--color-text-tertiary)\",fontSize:13}}>\n      A\u00fan no hay prompts guardados. Crea uno y gu\u00e1rdalo con \u2605\n    </div>\n  );\n  return (\n    <div style={{display:\"flex\",flexDirection:\"column\",gap:8}}>\n      {library.map((item,i)=>(\n        <div key={i} style={{background:\"var(--color-background-primary)\",border:\"0.5px solid var(--color-border-tertiary)\",borderRadius:12,padding:\"12px 14px\",display:\"flex\",flexDirection:\"column\",gap:6}}>\n          <div style={{display:\"flex\",alignItems:\"center\",gap:8}}>\n            <span style={{fontSize:14}}>{CATEGORIES.find(c=>c.id===item.category)?.icon||\"\ud83d\udcc4\"}</span>\n            <span style={{fontWeight:500,fontSize:13,color:\"var(--color-text-primary)\",flex:1}}>{item.name}</span>\n            <span style={{fontSize:10,color:\"var(--color-text-tertiary)\"}}>{item.date}</span>\n          </div>\n          <div style={{fontSize:11,color:\"var(--color-text-secondary)\",fontFamily:\"var(--font-mono)\",lineHeight:1.5,maxHeight:48,overflow:\"hidden\"}}>\n            {item.prompt}\n          </div>\n          <div style={{display:\"flex\",gap:6,marginTop:2}}>\n            <button onClick={()=>onLoad(item)} style={{padding:\"4px 12px\",borderRadius:20,border:\"0.5px solid #378ADD\",fontSize:11,cursor:\"pointer\",background:\"#E6F1FB\",color:\"#0C447C\"}}>\n              Cargar\n            </button>\n            <button onClick={()=>navigator.clipboard.writeText(item.prompt)} style={{padding:\"4px 12px\",borderRadius:20,border:\"0.5px solid var(--color-border-secondary)\",fontSize:11,cursor:\"pointer\",background:\"transparent\",color:\"var(--color-text-secondary)\"}}>\n              Copiar\n            </button>\n            <button onClick={()=>onDelete(i)} style={{padding:\"4px 12px\",borderRadius:20,border:\"0.5px solid        </div>\n        </div>\n      ))}\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\n// \u2500\u2500\u2500 MAIN APP setPrompt(parts.join(\", \"));\n  },[values, timeline, category, manualEdit]);\n\n  const toggle = (key, val, single) => {\n    setManualEdit(false);\n    setValues(prev => {\n      const cur = prev[key] || (single ? \"\" : []);\n      if(single) return {...prev, [key]: cur===val ? \"\" : val};\n      const arr = Array.isArray(cur) ?      transition:\\\"all .12s\\\",\\n      }}\\n    >{label}</button>\\n  );\\n}\\n\\nfunction Section({ sec, values, customTags, onToggle, onAddCustom }) {\\n  const [inp, setInp] =   {isMulti ? \\\"m\\u00faltiple\\\" : \\\"uno\\\"}\\n        </span>\\n      </div>\\n      <div style={{display:\\\"flex\\\",flexWrap:\\\"wrap\\\",gap:5,marginBottom:8}}>\\n        {allChoices.map(v \\u2014 ${beat.description||\\\"\\\"}${beat.notes?\\\" | \\\"+beat.notes:\\\"\\\"}`;\\n        parts.push(t.trim());\\n      });\\n    }\\n    setPrompt(parts.join(\\\", \\\"));\\n  },[values, };\\n\\n  const saveToLibrary = async () => {\\n    if(!prompt.trim()) return;\\n    const name = libName.trim() || `Prompt ${new cat=null;\nlet manualEdit=false;\nlet lang='ES';\nlet library=[];\nlet currentView='builder';\n\nasync function initLib(){\n  try{\n    const r=await window.storage?.get('prompt_library_v2');\n    if(r?.value){library=JSON.parse(r.value);updateLibCount();}\n  }catch(e){}\n}\n\nasync function persistLib(){\n  try{await window.storage?.set('prompt_library_v2',JSON.stringify(library));}catch(e){}\n}\n\nfunction updateLibCount(){\n  document.getElementById('lib-count').textContent=library.length;\n}\n\nfunction switchTab(v){\n  currentView=v;\n  document.getElementById('view-builder').style.display=v==='builder'?'':'none';\n  document.getElementById('view-library').style.display=v==='library'?'':'none';\n  document.querySelectorAll('.tab-btn').forEach(b=>b.classList.remove('active'));\n  document.getElementById('tab-'+v).classList.add('active');\n  if(v==='library')renderLib();\n}\n\nfunction renderCatPicker(){\n  const el=document.getElementById('cat-picker');\n  if(cat){el.style.display='none';document.getElementById('cat-header').style.display='flex';return;}\n  el.style.display='';\n  document.getElementById('cat-header').style.display='none';\n  el.innerHTML=`<div style=\"font-size:13px;color:var(--color-text-secondary);margin-bottom:14px\">\u00bfQu\u00e9 quieres crear?</div>\n  <div class=\"cat-grid\">${CATS.map(c=>`<button class=\"cat-card\" onclick=\"pickCat('${c.id}')\">\n    <span class=\"cat-icon\">${c.icon}</span>\n    <span class=\"cat-name\">${c.name}</span>\n    <span class=\"cat-desc\">${c.desc}</span>\n  </button>`).join('')}</div>`;\n}\n\nfunction pickCat(id){\n  cat=id;\n  const c=CATS.find(x=>x.id===id);\n  document.getElementById('cat-icon').textContent=c.icon;\n  document.getElementById('cat-name').textContent=c.name;\n  document.getElementById('cat-picker').style.display='none';\n  document.getElementById('cat-header').style.display='flex';\n  document.getElementById('timeline-wrap').style.display=id==='video'?'':'none';\n  renderSections();\n  buildPrompt();\n}\n\nfunction resetCat(){\n  cat=null;\n  Object.keys(state).forEach(k=>delete state[k]);\n  Object.keys(customs).forEach(k=>delete customs[k]);\n  timeline.length=0;\n  manualEdit=false;lang='ES';\n  renderCatPicker();\n  renderSections();\n  renderTimeline();\n  setPrompt('');\n  document.getElementById('lang-badge').textContent='ES';\n  document.querySelectorAll('.lang-btn').forEach(b=>b.classList.remove('active'));\n  document.getElementById('lang-es').classList.add('active');\n}\n\nfunction resetAll(){resetCat();}\n\nfunction renderSections(){\n  const el=document.getElementById('sections');\n  if(!cat){el.innerHTML='';return;}\n  const secs=SECS[cat]||[];\n  el.innerHTML=secs.map(sec=>{\n    const multi=!sec.single;\n    const cur=state[sec.key]||(multi?[]:null);\n    const cus=customs[sec.key]||[];\n    const all=[...sec.choices,...cus];\n    const cc=sec.color;\n    return `<div style=\"margin-bottom:18px\">\n      <div class=\"sec-label\">${sec.label}<span class=\"multi-badge\">${multi?'m\u00faltiple':'uno'}</span></div>\n      <div class=\"btn-grid\">${all.map(v=>{\n        const sel=multi?(Array.isArray(cur)&&cur.includes(v)):cur===v;\n        const isCustom=cus.includes(v);\n        return `<button class=\"tag-btn${isCustom?' custom':''} ${sel?'s-'+cc:''}\" onclick=\"toggleTag('${sec.key}','${v.replace(/'/g,\"\\\\'\")}',${!multi})\">${v}</button>`;\n      }).join('')}</div>\n      <div class=\"custom-row\">\n        <input class=\"custom-inp\" id=\"ci-${sec.key}\" placeholder=\"+ Tu propio bot\u00f3n...\" onkeydown=\"if(event.key==='Enter'){addCustom('${sec.key}',this);this.value=''}\">\n        <button class=\"add-btn\" onclick=\"addCustomBtn('${sec.key}')\">+ A\u00f1adir</button>\n      </div>\n    </div>`;\n  }).join('');\n}\n\nfunction toggleTag(key,val,single){\n  manualEdit=false;\n  if(single){state[key]=state[key]===val?null:val;}\n  else{\n    if(!Array.isArray(state[key]))state[key]=[];\n    const i=state[key].indexOf(val);\n    if(i>-1)state[key].splice(i,1);else state[key].push(val);\n  }\n  renderSections();\n  buildPrompt();\n}\n\nfunction addCustom(key,inp){\n  const val=inp.value.trim();\n  if(!val)return;\n  if(!customs[key])customs[key]=[];\n  if(!customs[key].includes(val)){customs[key].push(val);toggleTag(key,val,false);}\n  else{inp.value='';renderSections();}\n}\n\nfunction addCustomBtn(key){\n  const inp=document.getElementById('ci-'+key);\n  addCustom(key,inp);\n  inp.value='';\n}\n\nfunction addBeat(){\n  const last=timeline[timeline.length-1];\n  const start=last?Number(last.end):0;\n  timeline.push({start,end:start+3,desc:'',shot:'',move:'',light:'',emotion:'',lens:'',transition:'',notes:''});\n  manualEdit=false;\n  renderTimeline();\n  buildPrompt();\n}\n\nfunction updateBeat(i,field,val){\n  timeline[i][field]=val;\n  manualEdit=false;\n  buildPrompt();\n}\n\nfunction removeBeat(i){\n  timeline.splice(i,1);\n  manualEdit=false;\n  renderTimeline();\n  buildPrompt();\n}\n\nfunction miniBtn(i,field,val,color){\n  const renderTimeline(){\n  const el=document.getElementById('timeline');\n  if(!el)return;\n  if(timeline.length===0){el.innerHTML='<div class=\"empty-state\">A\u00f1ade tu primer momento en el timeline buildPrompt(){\n  if(manualEdit)return;\n  if(!cat){setPrompt('');return;}\n  const parts=[];\n  (SECS[cat]||[]).forEach(sec=>{\n    const v=state[sec.key];\n S = {\n  cat: null,\n  vals: {},\n  customs: {},\n  timeline: [],\n  prompt: \"\",\n  manualEdit: false,\n  lang: \"ES\",\n  library: [],\n  settings: {\n    theme: \"auto\",\n    defaultLang: \"ES\",\n    defaultPlatform: \"\",\n    defaultRatio: \"\",\n    activeCats: [\"app\",\"character\",\"location\",\"storyboard\",\"video\",\"sound\",\"music\"],\n    fontFamily: \"DM Sans\",\n    fontSize: 13,\n    density: \"normal\",\n  }\n};\n\n// \u2500\u2500 THEME \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\nfunction applyTheme(t) {\n  if (t === \"auto\") {\n    const h = new Date().getHours();\n    t = (h >= 7 && h < 19) ? \"light\" : \"dark\";\n  }\n  document.documentElement.setAttribute(\"data-theme\", t === \"dark\" ? \"dark\" : \"\");\n}\nfunction setTheme(t) {\n  S.settings.theme = t;\n  applyTheme(t);\n  document.querySelectorAll(\".theme-btn\").forEach(b => b.classList.remove(\"active\"));\n  document.getElementById(\"th-\" + t).classList.add(\"active\");\n  persist();\n}\n\n// \u2500\u2500 FONTS & DENSITY \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\nfunction applyFont(f, sz) {\n  document.documentElement.style.setProperty(\"--font-body\", `'${f}', sans-serif`);\n  document.documentElement.style.setProperty(\"--size-base\", sz + \"px\");\n}\nfunction applyDensity(d) {\n  const map = { compact: \"0.6rem\", normal: \"0.85rem\", spacious: \"1.1rem\" };\n  document.querySelectorAll(\".tag-group\").forEach(el => el.style.marginBottom = \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\nfunction persist() {\n  try { localStorage.setItem(\"pbp_state\", JSON.stringify({ library: S.library, settings: S.settings })); } catch(e) {}\n}\nfunction load() {\n  try {\n    const d = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(\"pbp_state\") || \"{}\");\n    if (d.library) S.library = d.library;\n    if (d.settings) Object.assign(S.settings, d.settings);\n  } catch(e) {}\n}\n\n// \u2500\u2500 VIEWS \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\nfunction switchView(v, btn) {\n  [\"builder\",\"library\",\"settings\"].forEach(id => {\n    document.getElementById(\"view-\" + id).style.display = id === v ? \"\" : \"none\";\n  });\n  document.querySelectorAll(\".nav-item\").forEach(b => b.classList.remove(\"active\"));\n  if (btn) btn.classList.add(\"active\");\n  if (v === \"library\") renderLib();\n  if (v === \"settings\") renderSettings();\n}\n\n// \u2500\u2500 CATEGORIES \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\nfunction renderCats() {\n  const grid = document.getElementById(\"cat-grid\");\n  const active = S.settings.activeCats;\n  grid.innerHTML = CATS.filter(c => active.includes(c.id)).map(c => `\n    <button class=\"cat-card ${S.cat === c.id ? 'active' : ''}\" onclick=\"pickCat('${c.id}')\">\n      <span class=\"cat-initial\">${c.initial}</span>\n      <span class=\"cat-name\">${c.name}</span>\n      <span class=\"cat-desc\">${c.desc}</span>\n    </button>\n  `).join(\"\");\n}\n\nfunction pickCat(id) {\n  S.cat = id;\n  S.vals = {};\n  S.customs = {};\n  S.timeline = [];\n  S.manualEdit = false;\n  if (S.settings.defaultPlatform && id === \"video\") S.vals[\"vid_platform\"] = S.settings.defaultPlatform;\n  if (S.settings.defaultRatio && id === \"video\") S.vals[\"vid_ratio\"] = S.settings.defaultRatio;\n  document.getElementById(\"cat-section\").style.display = \"none\";\n  document.getElementById(\"params-section\").style.display = \"\";\n  document.getElementById(\"active-cat-label\").textContent = CATS.find(c => c.id === id)?.name || \"\";\n  document.getElementById(\"timeline-section\").style.display = id === \"video\" ? \"\" : \"none\";\n  renderParams();\n  buildPrompt();\n}\n\nfunction clearCat() {\n  S.cat = null;\n  document.getElementById(\"cat-section\").style.display = \"\";\n  document.getElementById(\"params-section\").style.display = \"none\";\n  renderCats();\n  setPromptText(\"\");\n}\n\n// \u2500\u2500 PARAMS \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\nfunction renderParams() {\n  const body = document.getElementById(\"params-body\");\n  if (!S.cat) { body.innerHTML = \"\"; return; }\n  body.innerHTML = (SECS[S.cat] || []).map(sec => renderSection(sec)).join(\"\");\n}\n\nfunction renderSection(sec) {\n  const multi = !sec.single;\n  const cur = S.vals[sec.key] || (multi ? [] : null);\n  const cus = S.customs[sec.key] || [];\n  const all = [...sec.choices, ...cus];\n  return `<div class=\"tag-group\">\n    <div class=\"tag-group-label\">${sec.label} <span class=\"badge\">${multi ? \"m\u00faltiple\" : \"uno\"}</span></div>\n    <div class=\"tags\">${all.map(v => {\n      const sel = multi ? (Array.isArray(cur) && cur.includes(v)) : cur === v;\n      const isC = cus.includes(v);\n      return `<button class=\"tag${isC ? \" custom\" : \"\"}${sel ? \" sel\" : \"\"}\" onclick=\"toggleTag('${sec.key}','${v.replace(/'/g,\"\\\\'\").replace(/\"/g,'&quot;')}',${!multi})\">${v}</button>`;\n    }).join(\"\")}</div>\n    <div class=\"add-row\">\n      <input class=\"add-inp\" id=\"ci-${sec.key}\" placeholder=\"+ A\u00f1adir opci\u00f3n personalizada...\" onkeydown=\"if(event.key==='Enter'){addCustom('${sec.key}');}\" >\n      <button class=\"btn-ghost\" onclick=\"addCustom('${sec.key}')\" style=\"font-size:0.72rem\">A\u00f1adir</button>\n    </div>\n  </div>`;\n}\n\nfunction toggleTag(key, val, single) {\n  S.manualEdit = false;\n  if (single) { S.vals[key] = S.vals[key] === val ? null : val; }\n  else {\n    if (!Array.isArray(S.vals[key])) S.vals[key] = [];\n    const i = S.vals[key].indexOf(val);\n    if (i > -1) S.vals[key].splice(i, 1); else S.vals[key].push(val);\n  }\n  renderParams();\n  buildPrompt();\n}\n\nfunction addCustom(key) {\n  const inp = document.getElementById(\"ci-\" + key);\n  const val = inp.value.trim();\n  if (!val) return;\n  if (!S.customs[key]) S.customs[key] = [];\n  if (!S.customs[key].includes(val)) {\n    S.customs[key].push(val);\n    if (!Array.isArray(S.vals[key])) S.vals[key] = [];\n    S.vals[key].push(val);\n  }\n  inp.value = \"\";\n  S.manualEdit = false;\n  renderParams();\n  buildPrompt();\n}\n\n// \u2500\u2500 TIMELINE \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\nfunction addBeat() {\n  const last = S.timeline[S.timeline.length - 1];\n  const start = last ? Number(last.end) : 0;\n  S.timeline.push({ start, end: start + 3, desc: \"\", shot: \"\", move: \"\", light: \"\", emotion: \"\", lens: \"\", trans: \"\", notes: \"\" });\n  S.manualEdit = false;\n  renderTimeline();\n}\n\nfunction updateBeat(i, f, v) { S.timeline[i][f] = v; S.manualEdit = false; buildPrompt(); }\nfunction removeBeat(i) { S.timeline.splice(i, 1); S.manualEdit = false; renderTimeline(); buildPrompt(); }\n\nfunction renderTimeline() {\n  const body = document.getElementById(\"timeline-body\");\n  if (!body) return;\n  if (S.timeline.length === 0) { body.innerHTML = `<div style=\"font-size:0.8rem;color:var(--text3);padding:1rem 0;text-align:center;border:1px dashed var(--border);border-radius:var(--radius-lg)\">A\u00f1ade tu primer beat con el bot\u00f3n de arriba</div>`; return; }\n  body.innerHTML = S.timeline.map((b, i) => `\n    <div class=\"beat\">\n      <button class=\"remove-beat\" onclick=\"removeBeat(${i})\">\u00d7</button>\n      <div class=\"beat-time-row\">\n        <span class=\"beat-pill\">${b.start}s \u2014 ${b.end}s</span>\n        <input class=\"time-inp\" type=\"number\" value=\"${b.start}\" min=\"0\" onchange=\"updateBeat(${i},'start',this.value);renderTimeline()\" title=\"Inicio (s)\">\n        <span style=\"font-size:0.72rem;color:var(--text3)\">\u2192</span>\n        <input class=\"time-inp\" type=\"number\" value=\"${b.end}\" min=\"0\" onchange=\"updateBeat(${i},'end',this.value);renderTimeline()\" title=\"Fin (s)\">\n      </div>\n      <textarea class=\"beat-desc\" placeholder=\"\u00bfQu\u00e9 sucede en este momento? Acci\u00f3n, movimiento, emoci\u00f3n, detalles narrativos...\" oninput=\"updateBeat(${i},'desc',this.value)\">${b.desc}</textarea>\n      <div class=\"beat-params\">\n        ${miniParam(i,\"shot\",\"Plano\",SHOTS)}\n        ${miniParam(i,\"move\",\"Movimiento c\u00e1mara\",MOVES)}\n        ${miniParam(i,\"light\",\"Iluminaci\u00f3n\",LIGHTS)}\n        ${miniParam(i,\"emotion\",\"Emoci\u00f3n del beat\",EMOTIONS)}\n        ${miniParam(i,\"lens\",\"Lente\",LENSES)}\n        ${miniParam(i,\"trans\",\"Transici\u00f3n de salida\",TRANSQ)}\n      </div>\n      <input class=\"beat-notes\" value=\"${b.notes||''}\" placeholder=\"Notas: FX visual, color grade, sonido clave, detalle narrativo...\" oninput=\"updateBeat(${i},'notes',this.value)\">\n    </div>\n  `).join(\"\");\n}\n\nfunction miniParam(i, field, label, opts) {\n  const b = S.timeline[i];\n  return `<div>\n    <div class=\"beat-param-label\">${label}</div>\n    <div class=\"mini-tags\">${opts.map(o => `<button class=\"mini-tag${b[field]===o?' sel':''}\" onclick=\"updateBeat(${i},'${field}','${o.replace(/'/g,\"\\\\'\")}');renderTimeline()\">${o}</button>`).join(\"\")}</div>\n  </div>`;\n}\n\n// \u2500\u2500 PROMPT \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\nfunction buildPrompt() {\n  if (S.manualEdit) return;\n  if (!S.cat) { setPromptText(\"\"); return; }\n  const parts = [];\n  (SECS[S.cat] || []).forEach(sec => {\n    const v = S.vals[sec.key];\n    if (!v || (Array.isArray(v) && !v.length)) return;\n    parts.push(Array.isArray(v) ? v.join(\", \") : v);\n  });\n  if (S.cat === \"video\" && S.timeline.length > 0) {\n    parts.push(\"\\n\\n[VIDEO TIMELINE]\");\n    S.timeline.forEach(b => {\n      const meta = [b.shot, b.move, b.lens, b.light, b.emotion, b.trans].filter(Boolean).join(\" | \");\n      parts.push(`[${b.start}s\u2013${b.end}s]${meta ? \" \" + meta : \"\"} \u2014 ${b.desc}${b.notes ? \" \u2022 \" + b.notes : \"\"}`);\n    });\n  }\n  setPromptText(parts.join(\", \"));\n}\n\nfunction setPromptText(txt) {\n  S.prompt = txt;\n  const el = document.getElementById(\"prompt-area\");\n  if (el) el.value = txt;\n  updateTokens(txt);\n}\n\nfunction onManualEdit() {\n  S.manualEdit = true;\n  S.prompt = document.getElementById(\"prompt-area\").value;\n  updateTokens(S.prompt);\n}\n\nfunction updateTokens(txt) {\n  const w = txt.trim().split(/\\s+/).filter(Boolean).length;\n  const el = document.getElementById(\"token-info\");\n  if (el) el.textContent = w ? }\\n\\n// \\u2500\\u2500 TRANSLATE \\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\nasync function translateTo(lng) {\\n  const txt = getPrompt().trim();\\n  if (!txt) return;\\n  const labels = { ES: \\\"Espa\\u00f1ol\\\", FR: \\\"Fran\\u00e7ais\\\", EN: \\\"English\\\" };\\n  document.querySelectorAll(\\\".lang-btn\\\").forEach(b => b.disabled = true);\\n  try {\\n    const r = await fetch(\\\"https://api.anthropic.com/v1/messages\\\", {\\n      method: \\\"POST\\\",\\n      headers: { \\\"Content-Type\\\": \\\"application/json\\\" },\\n      body: JSON.stringify({\\n        model: \\\"claude-sonnet-4-20250514\\\", max_tokens: 1500,\\n        system: `Translate the following generative AI prompt to ${labels[lng]}. document.querySelectorAll(\".nitem\").forEach(b=>b.classList.remove(\"active\"));\n  if(btn)btn.classList.add(\"active\");\n  if(v===\"library\")renderLib();\n </button>`).join(\"\");\n}\n\nfunction pickCat(id){\n  S.cat=id; S.vals={}; S.customs={}; S.timeline=[]; S.manualEdit=false;\n \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\n// Store refs by key to avoid full re-render on toggle\nfunction renderParams(){\n  const body=document.getElementById(\"params-body\");\n  if(!S.cat){body.innerHTML=\"\";return;}\n  body.innerHTML=(SECS[S.cat]||[]).map(sec=>renderSection(sec)).join(\"\");\n}\n\nfunction renderSection(sec){\n  const multi=!sec.single;\n  const cur=S.vals[sec.key]||(multi?[]:null);\n  const cus=S.customs[sec.key]||[];\n  const all=[...sec.ch,...cus];\n  const tags=all.map(v=>{\n    const sel=multi?(Array.isArray(cur)&&cur.includes(v)):cur===v;\n    const isC=cus.includes(v);\n    // encode index to avoid quote issues in onclick\n    const idx=all.indexOf(v);\n    return `<button class=\"tag${isC?\" custom\":\"\"}${sel?\" sel\":\"\"}\" data-key=\"${esc(sec.key)}\" data-idx=\"${idx}\" data-single=\"${!multi}\" onclick=\"handleTag(this)\">${v}</button>`;\n  }).join(\"\");\n  return `<div class=\"tg\" id=\"tg-${esc(sec.key)}\">\n    <div class=\"tgl\">${sec.label} <span class=\"badge\">${multi?\"m\u00faltiple\":\"uno\"}</span></div>\n    <div class=\"tags\" id=\"tags-${esc(sec.key)}\">${tags}</div>\n    <div class=\"add-row\">\n      <input class=\"add-inp\" id=\"ci-${esc(sec.key)}\" placeholder=\"+ A\u00f1adir opci\u00f3n personalizada...\" onkeydown=\"if(event.key==='Enter')addCustom('${esc(sec.key)}')\">\n      <button class=\"gbtn\" onclick=\"addCustom('${esc(sec.key)}')\" style=\"font-size:.72rem\">A\u00f1adir</button>\n    </div>\n  </div>`;\n}\n\nfunction esc(s){return s.replace(/[^a-z0-9_]/gi,\"_\")}\n\n// Key lookup table for tag handler (survives any char in values)\nconst TAG_VALUES={};\nfunction registerTagValues(cat){\n  (SECS[cat]||[]).forEach(sec=>{\n    const cus=S.customs[sec.key]||[];\n    TAG_VALUES[sec.key]=[...sec.ch,...cus];\n  });\n}\n\nfunction handleTag(btn){\n  const key=btn.dataset.key;\n  const idx=parseInt(btn.dataset.idx);\n  const single=btn.dataset.single===\"true\";\n  // get actual value from lookup\n  registerTagValues(S.cat);\n  const val=TAG_VALUES[key]?.[idx];\n  if(val===undefined)return;\n  S.manualEdit=false;\n  if(single){\n    S.vals[key]=S.vals[key]===val?null:val;\n  }else{\n    if(!Array.isArray(S.vals[key]))S.vals[key]=[];\n    const i=S.vals[key].indexOf(val);\n    if(i>-1)S.vals[key].splice(i,1);else S.vals[key].push(val);\n  }\n  // update only this section's tags\n  refreshSection(key);\n  buildPrompt();\n}\n\nfunction refreshSection(key){\n  const sec=(SECS[S.cat]||[]).find(s=>s.key===key);\n  if(!sec)return;\n  const multi=!sec.single;\n  const cur=S.vals[key]||(multi?[]:null);\n  const cus=S.customs[key]||[];\n  const all=[...sec.ch,...cus];\n  const container=document.getElementById(\"tags-\"+esc(key));\n  if(!container)return;\n  container.querySelectorAll(\".tag\").forEach((btn,idx)=>{\n    const v=all[idx];\n    if(v===undefined)return;\n    const sel=multi?(Array.isArray(cur)&&cur.includes(v)):cur===v;\n    btn.classList.toggle(\"sel\",sel);\n  });\n}\n\nfunction addCustom(key){\n  const inp=document.getElementById(\"ci-\"+key);\n  if(!inp)return;\n  const val=inp.value.trim();\n  if(!val)return;\n  if(!S.customs[key])S.customs[key]=[];\n  if(!S.customs[key].includes(val)){\n    S.customs[key].push(val);\n    if(!Array.isArray(S.vals[key]))S.vals[key]=[];\n    S.vals[key].push(val);\n  }\n  inp.value=\"\";\n  S.manualEdit=false;\n  // re-render only this section\n  const tg=document.getElementById(\"tg-\"+key);\n  if(tg){\n    const sec=(SECS[S.cat]||[]).find(s=>s.key===key);\n    if(sec)tg.outerHTML=renderSection(sec);\n  }\n  buildPrompt();\n}\n\n// \u2500\u2500 TIMELINE \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\nfunction addBeat(){\n  const last=S.timeline[S.timeline.length-1];\n  const start=last?Number(last.end):0;\n  S.timeline.push({start,end:start+3,desc:\"\",shot:\"\",move:\"\",light:\"\",emotion:\"\",lens:\"\",trans:\"\",notes:\"\"});\n  S.manualEdit=false;\n  renderTimeline(); buildPrompt();\n}\n\nfunction updateBeat(i,f,v){S.timeline[i][f]=v;S.manualEdit=false;buildPrompt();}\nfunction removeBeat(i){S.timeline.splice(i,1);S.manualEdit=false;renderTimeline();buildPrompt();}\n\nfunction renderTimeline(){\n  const body=document.getElementById(\"timeline-body\");\n  if(!body)return;\n  if(S.timeline.length===0){\n    body.innerHTML=`<div return;\n  }\n  body.innerHTML=S.timeline.map((b,i)=>`\n    <div class=\"beat\">\n      <button class=\"rmbtn\" onclick=\"removeBeat(${i})\">\u00d7</button>\n      <div class=\"beat-tr\">\n        <span class=\"beat-pill\">${b.start}s \u2014 ${b.end}s</span>\n        <input class=\"tinp\" type=\"number\" value=\"${b.start}\" min=\"0\" ${bpanel(i,\"shot\",\"Plano\",SHOTS)}\n        ${bpanel(i,\"move\",\"Movimiento c\u00e1mara\",MOVES)}\n        ${bpanel(i,\"light\",\"Iluminaci\u00f3n\",LIGHTS)}\n        ${bpanel(i,\"emotion\",\"Emoci\u00f3n del beat\",EMOTIONS)}\n        ${bpanel(i,\"lens\",\"Lente\",LENSES)}\n        ${bpanel(i,\"trans\",\"Transici\u00f3n de salida\",TRANSQ)}\n      </div>\n class=\"bpl\">${label}</div>\n    <div class=\"mt\">${opts.map((o,oi)=>`<button class=\"${b[field]===o?\"sel\":\"\"}\" onclick=\"setBeat(${i},'${field}',${oi})\">${o}</button>`).join(\"\")}</div>\n  </div>`;\n}\n\n// Lookup arrays indexed for timeline\nconst TL_OPTS={shot:SHOTS,move:MOVES,light:LIGHTS,emotion:EMOTIONS,lens:LENSES,trans:TRANSQ};\nfunction setBeat(i,field,oi){\n  const PROMPT \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\nfunction ${b.desc}${b.notes?\" \u2022 \"+b.notes:\"\"}`);\n    });\n  }\n  setPromptText(parts.join(\", \"));\n}\n\nfunction setPromptText(txt){\n  S.prompt=txt;\n  const document.getElementById(\"prompt-area\")?.value||\"\";}\n\n// \u2500\u2500 TRANSLATE \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\nasync function translateTo(lng){\n  const txt=getPrompt().trim();\n  if(!txt){toast(\"Genera un prompt primero.\");return;}\n  const labels={ES:\"Spanish\",FR:\"French\",EN:\"English\"};\n  document.querySelectorAll(\".lbtn\").forEach(b=>b.disabled=true);\n  try{\n    const r=await fetch(\"https://api.anthropic.com/v1/messages\",{\n      method:\"POST\",\n      headers:{\"Content-Type\":\"application/json\"},\n      body:JSON.stringify({\n        model:\"claude-sonnet-4-20250514\",max_tokens:1500,\n        system:`Translate the following generative AI prompt to ${labels[lng]}. Preserve all technical cinematographic terms, acronyms (ELS, CU, ECU, etc.), timestamps, and the [VIDEO TIMELINE] block structure. Return ONLY the translated text, nothing else.`,\n        messages:[{role:\"user\",content:txt}]\n      })\n    });\n    const d=await r.json();\n    if(d.error){throw new Error(d.error.message);}\n    const out=d.content?.find(b=>b.type===\"text\")?.text||txt;\n    document.getElementById(\"prompt-area\").value=out;\n    S.manualEdit=true; S.lang=lng;\n    document.getElementById(\"lang-badge\").textContent=lng;\n    document.querySelectorAll(\".lbtn\").forEach(b=>b.classList.remove(\"active\"));\n    document.getElementById(\"lb-\"+lng)?.classList.add(\"active\");\n    updateTok(out);\n    toast(\"Traducido a \"+{ES:\"Espa\u00f1ol\",FR:\"Fran\u00e7ais\",EN:\"English\"}[lng]);\n  }catch(e){\n    // Fallback: copy prompt to ask Claude externally\n    const msgs={ES:\"Traduce al espa\u00f1ol este prompt de IA generativa, preservando t\u00e9rminos t\u00e9cnicos cinematogr\u00e1ficos y la estructura [VIDEO TIMELINE]:\\n\\n\"+txt,FR:\"Traduis en fran\u00e7ais ce prompt d'IA g\u00e9n\u00e9rative, en pr\u00e9servant les termes cin\u00e9matographiques techniques et la structure [VIDEO TIMELINE]:\\n\\n\"+txt,EN:\"Translate to English this generative AI prompt, preserving all cinematographic technical terms and the [VIDEO TIMELINE] structure:\\n\\n\"+txt};\n    navigator.clipboard.writeText(msgs[lng]).catch(()=>{});\n    toast(\"API no disponible. Instrucci\u00f3n copiada \u2014 p\u00e9gala en Claude.\");\n  }\n  document.querySelectorAll(\".lbtn\").forEach(b=>b.disabled=false);\n}\n\n// \u2500\u2500 COPY / RESET \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\nfunction copyPrompt(){\n  const txt=getPrompt();\n  if(!txt){toast(\"No hay prompt que copiar.\");return;}\n  navigator.clipboard.writeText(txt).catch(()=>{});\n  toast(\"Prompt copiado al portapapeles\");\n  document.getElementById(\"copy-btn\").textContent=\"Copiado \u2713\";\n  setTimeout(()=>document.getElementById(\"copy-btn\").textContent=\"Copiar\",1500);\n}\n\nfunction resetAll(){\n  S.cat=null;S.vals={};S.customs={};S.timeline=[];S.manualEdit=false;S.lang=\"ES\";\n  document.getElementById(\"cat-section\").style.display=\"\";\n  document.getElementById(\"params-section\").style.display=\"none\";\n  document.getElementById(\"lang-badge\").textContent=\"ES\";\n  document.querySelectorAll(\".lbtn\").forEach(b=>b.classList.remove(\"active\"));\n  document.getElementById(\"lb-ES\")?.classList.add(\"active\");\n  renderCats(); setPromptText(\"\");\n}\n\n// \u2500\u2500 AI ACTIONS \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\nfunction aiAction(type){\n  const p=getPrompt();\n  if(!p.trim()){toast(\"Genera un prompt primero.\");return;}\n  const msgs={\n    refine:`Refina y expande este prompt para IA generativa con m\u00e1s especificidad t\u00e9cnica. Incluye t\u00e9rminos de fotograf\u00eda, direcci\u00f3n de arte, narrativa visual y cinematograf\u00eda. Dame la versi\u00f3n Pro completa:\\n\\n${p}`,\n    variations:`Dame 3 variaciones completamente distintas de este prompt, cada una con un enfoque est\u00e9tico diferente pero manteniendo la esencia. Numera cada variaci\u00f3n claramente:\\n\\n${p}`,\n    optimize:`Translate to English and optimize technically for generative AI platforms (FLUX, Midjourney, Sora, Kling, Seedance). Include advanced technical parameters and platform-specific syntax:\\n\\n${p}`,\n  };\n  navigator.clipboard.writeText(msgs[type]).catch(()=>{});\n  const labels={refine:\"Instrucci\u00f3n para ChatGPT\");\n}\n\n// \u2500\u2500 LIBRARY \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\nfunction savePrompt(){\n  const txt=getPrompt().trim();\n  if(!txt){toast(\"No hay prompt que guardar.\");return;}\n  const inp=document.getElementById(\"save-inp\");\n  const
